<h2 class="entry-title type-1"><a href="https://honestlywtf.com/diy/diy-drippy-nails/" rel="bookmark">DIY Drippy Nails</a></h2>

Halloween is one of our favorite holidays and thus, the timing couldn’t have been better to recreate this deliciously daring DIY for our phalanges.</p>

<div class="post-content">
<h2 class="entry-title type-1"><a href="https://honestlywtf.com/diy/diy-ice-dyeing/" rel="bookmark">DIY Ice Dyeing + Giveaway</a></h2>
<p>Ice dyeing. I’m officially obsessed. This unique method of tie dying has been on my to-do list for years – I’m actually surprised it’s taken me this long to finally have experimented with it! Zappos, who recently just launched a denim shop this fall, was actually the catalyst for this long overdue DIY. Did you know that ice dyeing was first conceived by quilters who were looking for a resourceful way to dye fabric during winter months? </p>
<div class="entry-footer flex">

<div class="featured-image">
<a href="https://honestlywtf.com/travel/visiting-venice-italy/">
<img alt="" class="attachment-full size-full" height="920" sizes="(max-width: 1220px) 100vw, 1220px" src="https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/venice_landscape.webp" srcset="https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/venice_landscape.webp 1220w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/venice_landscape-300x226.webp 300w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/venice_landscape-768x579.webp 768w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/venice_landscape-640x483.webp 640w" width="1220"/> </a>

<div class="post-content">
<div class="post-tag type-4">Travel</div>
<h2 class="entry-title"><a href="https://honestlywtf.com/travel/visiting-venice-italy/" rel="bookmark">Visiting Venice, Italy</a></h2>
<p>This summer, I had the opportunity to travel to Italy with <a href="https://www.instagram.com/p/BzOkLBqgqzA/">Aperol</a> to celebrate 100 years of one of my favorite summer aperitifs in its birthplace of Padova, Italy – just about an hour from Venice. And if you have never been to this oh-so-unique city, it is in one word: <em>magical</em>. Did you know that Venice is built on more than 100 small islands in a lagoon in the Adriatic Sea?</p>

<a href="https://honestlywtf.com/art/cj-hendrys-rorschach/">
<img alt="" class="attachment-full size-full" height="1300" sizes="(max-width: 980px) 100vw, 980px" src="https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/cjhendry19.webp" srcset="https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/cjhendry19.webp 980w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/cjhendry19-300x398.webp 300w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/cjhendry19-768x1019.webp 768w, https://honestlywtf.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/cjhendry19-640x849.webp 640w" width="980"/> </a>

<h2 class="entry-title type-1"><a href="https://honestlywtf.com/art/cj-hendrys-rorschach/" rel="bookmark">Cj Hendry’s Rorschach</a></h2>
<div class="fl-row fl-row-fixed-width fl-row-bg-none fl-node-5c0632b10999c intro" data-node="5c0632b10999c">
<div class="fl-row-content-wrap">
<div class="fl-row-content fl-row-fixed-width fl-node-content">
<div class="fl-col-group fl-node-5c0632b1098f2" data-node="5c0632b1098f2">
<div class="fl-col fl-node-5c0632b109932" data-node="5c0632b109932">
<div class="fl-col-content fl-node-content">
<div class="fl-module fl-module-rich-text fl-node-5c0632b109971" data-node="5c0632b109971">
<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content">
<div class="fl-rich-text">
<p>Bow down. The Queen of Hyperrealism has done it again. You might remember Cj Hendry’s <a href="https://honestlywtf.com/art/cj-hendrys-complimentary-colors/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Complimentary Colors</a>, a series of drawings of paint swatches using colored pencils. Yes, <em>colored pencils – </em>the same medium used to draw hyperrealistic depictions of blobs of colorful oil paint for her latest series: <a href="http://www.cjhendry.live/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Rorschach</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="fl-row fl-row-fixed-width fl-row-bg-none fl-node-5c060738aa914 img-text-box" data-node="5c060738aa914">
<div class="fl-row-content-wrap">
<div class="fl-row-content fl-row-fixed-width fl-node-content">
<div class="fl-col-group fl-node-5c060738aa7aa" data-node="5c060738aa7aa">
<div class="fl-col fl-node-5c060738aa825" data-node="5c060738aa825">
<div class="fl-col-content fl-node-content">
<div class="fl-module fl-module-photo fl-node-5c060738aa8d9" data-node="5c060738aa8d9">
<div class="fl-module-content fl-node-content"></div>
</div>
</div>

<h2 class="entry-title type-1"><a href="https://honestlywtf.com/home/shared-bedrooms-for-kids/" rel="bookmark">Shared Bedrooms For Kids</a></h2>
<p>My daughter Coco is almost 1 and a half and the transition to her own room is long overdue. Our house has three bedrooms, one of which we’d like to save for our guests – especially the grandparents, who travel to visit us frequently.</p>
